- **skillnotary-drift-watch**: Set up a recurring check that tells the user when an installed agent skill or DSH plugin changes after they approved it. Use when the user asks to watch, monitor, schedule or periodically verify skills or plugins; when they want to be told about upstream changes; or when they say things like 定时检查、定期审计、监控 skill 变化、每天帮我看看. - **skillops**: 技能人事专员「小能」—— 户主邱懿武的 AI 技能 HRD：盘点、落户、体检、考核、周报、遥测，及技能 HRD 方法论的权威表达。当用户说「存成技能」「保存为技能」「把这个做成 skill」「我的技能」「技能库」「花名册」「技能状态」「落户」「入册」「入编」「人事周报」「技能盘点」「整理我的技能」「这个技能怎么样了」「小能」「技能方法论」「SkillOps 怎么讲」，或当你刚替用户创建/修改了任何 SKILL.md 文件，或 SessionStart 注入了 [skillops 技能人事体检] 警报需要处置时，必须使用本技能。 ([qiuyiwu1989-star/qiuyiwu-skills](https://github.com/qiuyiwu1989-star/qiuyiwu-skills/tree/HEAD/skillops-library/skills/skillops--70260b0fc424d95e/versions/1) · ★1)
- **skillops**: 技能人事部（HRD）—— 把每个 AI 技能当员工管：入库、落户、盘点、评级(SMS)、图谱、培训提分、绩效退化预警、退役归档的全生命周期。当用户说「造一个技能」「造技能」「存成技能」「保存为技能」「审计一下 XX」「扫描一下技能库」「我的技能库」「花名册」「技能状态」「技能关系」「团队能力盘点」「帮 XX 提分到 A」「哪些技能在退化」「哪些技能该下线」「技能盘点」「整理我的技能」「技能评级」「SMS 评分」，或当你刚创建/修改了任何 SKILL.md 文件（出生即入册），或 SessionStart 注入了 [skillops 技能人事体检] 警报时，使用本技能。 ([qiuyiwu1989-star/qiuyiwu-skills](https://github.com/qiuyiwu1989-star/qiuyiwu-skills/tree/HEAD/skillops-library/skills/skill--13f3488c-0ce5-40b0-b35b-e03fe6c80762/versions/1.0.0) · ★1)

- **skillopt**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/skillopt) · ★745)
- **skillopt**: Runs the SkillOpt single-lineage optimization loop, which organizes a hill-climb into epochs over mini-batches of train tasks under a textual learning rate — an integer edit budget that decays on a constant|linear|cosine schedule — and ends each epoch with one extra gated consolidation step. Parent is always the current best; acceptance is the val significance gate. Use when a run should anneal from broad early edits to small late ones and consolidate once per epoch, rather than hill-climb's one-shot whole-trainset proposals or gepa's Pareto frontier. ([skillberry-ai/cap-evolve](https://github.com/skillberry-ai/cap-evolve/tree/HEAD/skills/algorithms/skillopt) · ★70)
- **skillopt**: Improve a skill, agent file, or prompt through bounded patches and a held-out, per-task acceptance gate. Use when you say `optimize this skill`, `make this skill better`, `compress these skills safely`, `tune this prompt against an eval`, or a skill keeps failing the same way. Do NOT use to create or route skills (use skillforge) or to decide skill placement or compress markdown without an eval (use context-optimizer). ([rjmurillo/ai-agents](https://github.com/rjmurillo/ai-agents/tree/HEAD/src/cursor/skills/skillopt) · ★49 · 6 copies)
- **skillopt**: Prepare or run Whetstone SkillOpt optimization when asked to optimize a process skill using SkillOpt. ([iliaal/whetstone](https://github.com/iliaal/whetstone/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/skillopt) · ★37)
- **skillopt**: Bounded text-space gradient optimization for SKILL.md prompts using execution traces. Loads low-reward traces (reward &lt; 0.8) from .agent/traces/, groups them by skill, and applies bounded edits (max 15% word change) with a Validation Gate to prevent destructive rewrites. Use when you want to run the SkillOpt algorithm to automatically improve skill prompts, or when a skill repeatedly underperforms and you want a systematic text-space optimization pass. Triggers: skillopt, skill optimization, bounded edits, prompt gradient, validation gate, improve skill ([LowyShin/giip-fde-agent](https://github.com/LowyShin/giip-fde-agent/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/skillopt) · ★34)
- **skillopt**: Run controlled skill optimization cycles on any skill document. Uses kanban-based pipelines with validation gates — the methodology from Microsoft Research's SkillOpt (arXiv 2605.23904). ([magnus919/hermes-SkillOpt](https://github.com/magnus919/hermes-SkillOpt/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★32 · 2 copies)
- **skillopt**: Use when the user asks about Microsoft SkillOpt, optimizing or training agent skill documents with reflective loops, evaluating skill changes with validation gates, or running SkillOpt experiments. This is a wrapper around the local reviewed source checkout and must not run installs, training, WebUI, or model/API calls unless explicitly requested. ([Undermybelt/hermes-skills](https://github.com/Undermybelt/hermes-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/research/skillopt) · ★11)

Use when a user wants to optimize SKILL.md, prompt procedures, OpenClaw/Hermes/Codex/Claude Code skills, agent workflows, skill factories, benchmark-driven skill iteration, rollout analysis, validation gates, best_skill.md export, or controlled self-evolving skills inspired by Microsoft SkillOpt. ([Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge/tree/HEAD/community/clawhub/s/skillopt) · ★3)

- **SkillOpt**: Microsoft's text-space optimizer that trains reusable natural-language skills for frozen LLM agents through trajectory-driven edits, validation-gated updates, and deployable best_skill.md artifacts. Use when the user wants to optimize agent prompts/skills, run SkillOpt training loops, evaluate skill artifacts, extend with new benchmarks or backends, or use SkillOpt-Sleep for nightly self-evolution. - **skillopt**: Use when a user wants to set up, configure, run, or learn SkillOpt — the text-space skill optimizer that trains agent skill documents like neural-net weights. Triggers on requests like "set up a SkillOpt run", "help me train a skill on my data", "configure SkillOpt for my benchmark", "add a new benchmark to SkillOpt", "what hyperparameters should I use", or "interpret my SkillOpt results". Drives a one-question-at-a-time interview that fills out a runnable config and teaches the deep-learning analogy behind every choice. ([emmahyde/dotfiles](https://github.com/emmahyde/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/skillopt))
- **skillopt**: Self-evolving skill optimization framework for AI agents based on Microsoft SkillOpt and OKF (Observable Knowledge Framework) standards. Optimizes natural-language SKILL.md documents using trajectory reflection, patch aggregation, bounded editing, and validation gating without model fine-tuning. ([fj906880l-web/web-and-bios-scrapper-](https://github.com/fj906880l-web/web-and-bios-scrapper-/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ops/modules/skillopt))
- **skillopt**: Meta-skill that wraps every other skill invocation with the SkillOpt discipline (Yang et al., Microsoft + SJTU + Tongji + Fudan, May 2026, arXiv:2605.23904v2). Treats each skill document as the trainable external state of a frozen agent and uses bounded add/delete/replace edits, a held-out validation gate, a rejected-edit buffer, and an epoch-wise slow/meta update to improve the skill over time. Load this skill on every future skill call — whenever the agent loads any user, space, org, or built-in skill, also load skillopt and run the SkillOpt loop around the target skill's execution.
